Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the geometric object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 522. The moduli space has dimension at most 727 with 5 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to geometric theory, we decompose the problem into 522 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 727. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
